判定刚通过，项目从 MVP 验证进入 **M1' bootstrap CI + cross-model** 阶段。\n\n**已完成核心发现**：importance eviction 下 phase severity gradient 单调递增 12.8x→28.7x→52.9x→74.9x（4 个 sparsity level），random baseline 恒定 ~1x。Crossover severity)→D015/D016(GO)\n- **核心 claim**：importance-based compound sparsity 的交互严重度是 phase-dependent 的（gradient 12.8x-74.9x），random 不是（~1x）。这意味着 phase-gated controller 有理论依据\n- **Entropy-Discrimination Collapse 机制**：head pruning → surviving heads 注意力更均匀 → importance score 区分度崩溃 → importance eviction 在 low-sparsity phase 退化为有偏伪随机。这是机制解释，Director 要求论文必须包含\n- **截稿 2026-05-25（11 天）**，GPU 预算 ~250h 已用 ~2.9h\n- **文献空白已确认**：3 个 Worker 独立验证无先前工作报告 phase-dependent strategy crossover\n- **易踩坑**：(1) 不要把 v4 系列的 II 数据当真——全部 invalidated；(2) mean 和 median 在 s≥0.25 时差异巨大，混用会自相矛盾；(3) Qwen2.5-7B（base）和 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 是不同模型，cross-model 用的是 Instruct 版本（Worker 正在准备）；(4) 4 张 GPU 全空着 = 失职，接手后立即推进提交\n\n## 可查的历史\n前 12 轮 handoff 在 `task_context/history/agent/loop_*.md`，如有疑问可 Read 回查。\n完整聊天归档可用 [HANDOFF] 你是 Main Agent，这是 Loop #3。

## 上一轮交接 (task_context)

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- GPU 白名单：**westd-22162 cuda:0~3**，严禁用其他设备
- 服务器是 **Blackwell sm_100+**（非 Hopper），M4 kernel / M5 throughput 阶段必须短租 H20
- 环境激活**必须**用 `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`，**不存在 .venv**
- s=0.30 存在 heavy tail（mean/median=43:1），论文/汇报**只能锚定 median**，不得用 mean 做主叙事
- v4 及之前的 importance II 值**全部无效**（NaN 中毒 + 位置偏差），仅 v5 开头的实验可引用
- Director 明确要求：描述为 "emergent phase-strategy interaction"，**禁止** "importance metric limitation" 叙事，**禁止** fallback 思维

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：**westd-22162**，4× RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition 96GB
- 工作目录：`/root/autodl-tmp/pacs`
- Python：`/root/miniconda3/bin/python`（conda base）
- 模型：`/root/autodl-tmp/pacs/checkpoints/DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B`
- Qwen2.5-7B（cross-model 用）：`/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen2.5-7B/`
- 数据：`/root/autodl-tmp/pacs/data/math500.json`（50 题）
- 代码修复 commit：`20182ec`（NaN skip + position normalization + Layer 27 NaN skip）
- 活跃进程：**无 GPU 实验在跑**，4 张卡全空闲
- 活跃 Worker：2 个 running
  - `worker_code_p0b-qwen-instruct-prep`：准备 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 的 cross-model 验证
  - `worker_code_p0-per-problem-ii-fix`：修复 per-problem II 计算
- QUEUED 实验：`mvp_v5_random_s020`（cuda:0）、`mvp_v5_random_s030`（cuda:1）—— 未提交
- 活跃 Cron：0

### 3. 📊 当前状态
D016 GO 判定刚通过，项目从 MVP 验证进入 **M1' bootstrap CI + cross-model** 阶段。

**已完成核心发现**：importance eviction 下 phase severity gradient 单调递增 12.8x→28.7x→52.9x→74.9x（4 个 sparsity level），random baseline 恒定 ~1x。Crossover 现象：s=0.25 时 importance 在 high phase 优于 random（0.584 vs 3.002），在 low phase 劣于 random（30.9 vs 2.9）。

**Registry 状态**：28 实验总计。DONE 26（success 5 / negative 7 / failed 11 / killed 3），QUEUED 2。

**等待中**：
- 2 个 Worker 回调（Qwen prep + per-problem II fix）
- QUEUED 的 s020/s030 random baselines 尚未提交（补全 D015 对照数据）

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
- Worker `p0b-qwen-instruct-prep` 完成 → 提交 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct cross-model profiling（4 sparsity × importance+random = 8 runs，4 GPU 并行分两批）
- Worker `p0-per-problem-ii-fix` 完成 → 用修复后代码重算 v5 已有实验的 per-problem II，生成 bootstrap CI
- 上述两 Worker 都完成 → 提交 QUEUED 的 `mvp_v5_random_s020` + `mvp_v5_random_s030`（补全 random baseline 矩阵）
- Cross-model 结果出炉 → 若 Qwen 也显示 phase gradient >> 1x → claim 推广到 "model-agnostic"；若不显示 → 限缩 claim scope 为 DeepSeek 系列
- M1' 全部完成 → 向 Director 汇报 M1' 结果 + M2 per-phase grid search 方案

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
- **决策链**：D001(MVP)→D007-D009(三轮失败)→D010(pivot 1: strategy reversal)→D013(坏指标)→D014(pivot 2: phase-modulated severity)→D015/D016(GO)
- **核心 claim**：importance-based compound sparsity 的交互严重度是 phase-dependent 的（gradient 12.8x-74.9x），random 不是（~1x）。这意味着 phase-gated controller 有理论依据
- **Entropy-Discrimination Collapse 机制**：head pruning → surviving heads 注意力更均匀 → importance score 区分度崩溃 → importance eviction 在 low-sparsity phase 退化为有偏伪随机。这是机制解释，Director 要求论文必须包含
- **截稿 2026-05-25（11 天）**，GPU 预算 ~250h 已用 ~2.9h
- **文献空白已确认**：3 个 Worker 独立验证无先前工作报告 phase-dependent strategy crossover
- **易踩坑**：(1) 不要把 v4 系列的 II 数据当真——全部 invalidated；(2) mean 和 median 在 s≥0.25 时差异巨大，混用会自相矛盾；(3) Qwen2.5-7B（base）和 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 是不同模型，cross-model 用的是 Instruct 版本（Worker 正在准备）；(4) 4 张 GPU 全空着 = 失职，接手后立即推进提交

    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

Random II 在相同 sparsity 下 95% CI 下界 > 0（显著 super-additive）\\n3. 至少一个额外模型（Qwen2.5-7B）也观察到符号反转趋势\\n\\n## 近期任务\\n- [ ] 修复 `cumulative_attention_received` 位置偏差：`importance[j] /= (seq_len - j)`\\n- [ ] 修复后 sanity check：打印修复前后 top-10 eviction 位置确认不再系统性驱逐末尾\\n- [ ] II 统计量增加 median + winsorized mean + skewness + outlier count\\n- [ ] 修复后重跑 s=0.20 (cuda:0) 验证\\n- [ ] 修复后重跑 s=0.30 (cuda:1) 验证\\n- [ ] 根据修复后结果决定：strategy-dependent reversal 是否成立 → 继续 pivot / 再次 pivot\\n\\n## Post-MVP Milestones（暂定，修复结果出来后重估）\\n\\n| # | 阶段 | GPU-hours | 依赖 |\\n|---|------|-----------|------|\\n| M1' | Strategy reversal bootstrap + cross-model | ~8h | 指标修复验证通过 |\\n| M2 | Per-phase grid search | ~30h | M1' pass |\\n| M3 | Controller training | ~7h | M2 |\\n| M4 | Kernel integration | ~25h | M1' |\\n| M5 | Full evaluation | ~80h | M3+M4 |\\n| M6 | Ablation suite | ~40h | M5 |\\n| M7 | Validation (Qwen3-8B) | ~10h | M3+M4 |\\n\\n## GPU 预算\\n- RTX 6000 Pro: ~250h 预算，westd-22162 cuda:0~3，已用 ~2.9h\\n- H100 短租: ~2-4h，M5 阶段向 Manager 申请\\n\\n## 关键决策日志\\n\\n| ID | 日期 | 摘要 | 关联实验 |\\n|----|------|------|----------|\\n| D016 | 2026-05-14 | D015: GO — Phase-modulated severity claim 验证通过，进入  | mvp_v5_fixed_s015, mvp_v5_fixed_s020_r2, mvp_v5_fixed_s025, mvp_v5_fixed_s030_r2, mvp_v5_random_s015, mvp_v5_random_s025 |\\n| D015 | 2026-05-14 | GO: Phase-modulated severity confirmed (median gra | mvp_v5_fixed_s015, mvp_v5_fixed_s020_r2, mvp_v5_fixed_s025, mvp_v5_fixed_s030_r2, mvp_v5_random_s015, mvp_v5_random_s025 |\\n| D014 | 2026-05-14 | D010 pivot hypothesis NEGATIVE; ```text
Let me check the resource-saturation skill path and get worker status for both workers. I also need to check the project config for GPU allocation.

The QUEUED experiments have spec but their status is QUEUED, not ALLOCATED. `submit_on_allocated` requires ALLOCATED status. So I need to first allocate them (which requires Director approval via the PENDING flow).

Wait, let me re-read the state machine: PREPARING → PENDING（审批）→ QUEUED → ALLOCATED → RUNNING → ANALYZING → DONE

So QUEUED → ALLOCATED requires GPU allocation by Director. 

These experiments are already QUEUED with GPU assignments specified (cuda:0 and cuda:1), but they're not yet ALLOCATED. The `submit_on_allocated` tool requires ALLOCATED status, which means I need to check the allocate tool to see if I can transition them myself or if the Director needs to do it.
```
